## 2026-07-30 — RES-003A: DEC-SEC-001 Founder Decision Review Package

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Task:** prepare a Founder Decision Review Package for `DEC-SEC-001`, per the Founder-authorized task "TASK — RES-003A: DEC-SEC-001 Founder Decision Preparation." Prepares a decision *session* only — does not record or approve `DEC-SEC-001`, introduces no new engineering recommendation beyond `RES-003`, and does not modify `DEC-PROV-004`, application code, or repository architecture.
- **Impact of `DEC-PROV-004`'s confirmation analyzed first (required by the brief):** the constitutional Progressive Trust Model and Progressive Authentication principles reframe `DEC-SEC-001`'s fallback question from a delivery-mechanism choice into a product-philosophy question — what order alternatives are offered in, whether phone verification is still required later, when human-assisted registration appears, and what experience principles govern recovery. `RES-003`'s own corrected finding (the identity-resolution flow for "first OTP fails" is genuinely undesigned) is why these product questions must be answered before that flow can be designed — engineering cannot supply the answers itself.
- **Repository grounding used:** TRD12 §12.30 ("Account Recovery") and §12.31 ("Lost Phone Number") were found to already define a mature recovery journey for an *existing* customer who later loses phone access, but no repository document defines the equivalent for a *prospective* customer whose first verification attempt fails — confirming this is a genuine, undesigned gap, not an oversight. PRD2's existing Registration Journey (Steps 1–7) and Account Status vocabulary (including "Pending Verification") were reused to model the Fallback Journey as a branch, not a redesign. *Moments That Matter* §1's existing "ease, not scrutiny" and Progressive KYC framing, and PRD2 §2's existing design objectives (including "recoverable"), were used to ground Founder Decision Question 5 rather than inventing new principles.
- **Five Founder Decision Questions presented, per the brief's explicit instruction not to recommend an answer:** (1) fallback order — four repository-supported sequences presented neutrally; (2) whether Google/Email-authenticated customers must later verify their phone before identity-protected capabilities unlock; (3) when assisted registration becomes available; (4) whether merchants may participate in assisted identity verification, and under what principles (considerations only, no implementation design); (5) what customer-experience principles should govern identity recovery.
- **Impact Assessment provided for each question** across customer experience, engineering complexity, security, support operations, and future extensibility, drawn from existing `RES-001`/`RES-003`/TRD12/PRD2 evidence — no new technical claims introduced.
- **Decision readiness:** not yet ready to record `DEC-SEC-001` — the engineering recommendation and question framing are complete, but the Founder's actual answers to the five questions are required before a future task can incorporate them and route the decision for countersign.
- **Validation performed:** confirmed engineering recommendations remain unchanged from `RES-003` (re-read directly, no edits made to that document); confirmed no governance decision was recorded (Decision Register untouched); confirmed constitutional principles remain consistent with `DEC-PROV-004` (re-read directly, no edits made to that document or its Register entry); confirmed customer-journey content is clearly separated from engineering analysis throughout (§4 explicitly labeled "a decision model, not implementation logic"); `npx prettier --check` clean; `git status --short` confirmed only the intended two files changed.
- **Files created:** `docs/00-governance/decisions/evidence/DEC-SEC-001-founder-decision-review-package-2026-07-30.md`; `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Files modified:** none.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none introduced — Founder-review preparation only; no engineering recommendation changed, no governance document recorded, no code or architecture changed; `DEC-SEC-001` and `DEC-PROV-004` both remain exactly as they were before this task.

## 2026-07-30 — RES-003B: DEC-SEC-001 Founder Decision Recording

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Task:** record the Founder-approved decision for `DEC-SEC-001` in the live Decision Register, using the Founder decisions agreed during the `RES-003A` review session, per the Founder task "TASK — RES-003B: DEC-SEC-001 Founder Decision Recording." A governance-recording task only — no engineering recommendation changed, no implementation authorized, `RES-003`'s engineering package and `DEC-PROV-004` both left unmodified.
- **Recording strategy stated before editing (per the task's own requirement):** convert `DEC-SEC-001`'s Status to `CONFIRMED`, record the Founder's five decisions verbatim in `Final decision`, leave `Options identified`/`Recommended direction` untouched (engineering framing, not to be altered), disclose the `EXT-TECH-001` evidence gap as a launch-readiness matter rather than silently dropping it, and flag downstream tracker synchronization (Programme/Master Workflow/RTM/`CDR-001`) as an explicit follow-on item outside this task's narrow scope — consistent with the precedent `ENG-P2-RES-ADMIN-002` established for `DEC-PROV-004`'s equivalent sync.
- **Decision recorded, verbatim:** (1) **Authentication Recovery Order** — SMS OTP → Retry/Resend → Google Sign-In → Email Verification → Assisted Support; (2) **Progressive Phone Verification** — phone verification is part of establishing customer identity but not a universal onboarding blocker; the platform may progressively request it and require it before identity-protected capabilities (e.g., reward redemption); (3) **Merchant Assistance** — merchants may assist with onboarding/recovery as customer support, but do not verify identity; identity authority remains with the platform; (4) **Identity Recovery** — recovery restores an existing identity, never creates a replacement; recovered customers retain loyalty participation, purchase history, rewards, recognition, trust level, and customer history; (5) **Identity Recovery Principles** — 8 principles recorded exactly as given (identity belongs to the customer; recovery restores the same identity; never creates a duplicate; loyalty participation continues; verification requirements increase progressively by risk; recovery should be simple enough for independent completion; every recovery action must be auditable; protected capabilities may require verified identity regardless of earlier use). `Decision date: 2026-07-30`; `Approved by: Founder`.
- **Engineering recommendation preserved unaltered:** `RES-003`'s own decision package (Option A — email link, with the identity-resolution flow disclosed as an undesigned prerequisite) was not modified by this recording — the Register's `Options identified`/`Recommended direction` fields were left exactly as `RES-003` left them, per this task's explicit constraint.
- **`DEC-PROV-004` unmodified:** re-confirmed unchanged (`CONFIRMED`, its own 9 principles untouched) before and after this recording.
- **Register Summary (§5) recomputed:** `CONFIRMED` 39 → 40; `OPEN_ENGINEERING` 15 → 14; total unchanged (103). Front-matter "Last controlled update" line updated to record this action.
- **`RES-003A` Founder Decision Review Package updated:** §7 (Decision Readiness) appended with a "Recording status update (`RES-003B`)" note confirming the Founder's answers to Questions 1–5 have been recorded and that `RES-003`'s engineering recommendation was preserved unmodified — the identity-resolution flow's own design work remains a distinct, not-yet-performed future task.
- **Not performed, explicitly flagged as follow-on:** downstream tracking artefacts (Engineering Implementation Programme, Master Workflow, Requirements Traceability Matrix, `CDR-001`) still describe `DEC-SEC-001` as open — syncing these is outside this task's narrow "record Founder decisions only" scope and its "do not modify unrelated files" constraint, per the same precedent established for `DEC-PROV-004`'s equivalent sync (`ENG-P2-RES-ADMIN-002`). The identity-resolution flow `RES-003` §9 flagged as an undesigned prerequisite remains undesigned — this recording did not design it, only recorded the principles it must now be designed against.
- **Validation performed:** live re-read of `DEC-SEC-001`'s Decision Register entry, the corrected `RES-003` decision package, and `RES-003A`'s Founder Review Package before editing; confirmed engineering recommendations unchanged (direct re-read, no edits to `RES-003`'s own document); confirmed no other governance decision recorded (only `DEC-SEC-001`'s entry touched); confirmed `DEC-PROV-004` unchanged (direct re-read of its Register entry and decision package); confirmed customer-journey/philosophy content stays distinguishable from engineering analysis (the recorded principles reference `RES-003A`'s own separation, not `RES-003`'s technical text); `npx prettier --check` on both modified files, clean; `git status --short`/`git diff --stat` confirmed only the two intended files changed (15 insertions, 13 deletions across the Register; 2 insertions to the Founder Review Package).

## 2026-07-30 — RES-005: DEC-DATA-007 Dependency & Scope Analysis

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Task:** perform a structured dependency and scope analysis for `DEC-DATA-007` ("Loyalty number and QR reference generation") before its engineering decision package is prepared, per the Founder-authorized task "TASK — RES-005: DEC-DATA-007 Dependency & Scope Analysis." Analysis only — no decision package produced, no Founder decision requested, no implementation authorized, no application code modified.
- **Central finding:** a scope-framing discrepancy between this task's own background (describing `DEC-DATA-007` as a "foundational decision" of comparable character to `DEC-PROV-004`/`DEC-SEC-001`/`DEC-ID-003`) and the live Decision Register entry plus the Resolution Plan's own `RES-007` work package (Engineering-Lead-owned, `Founder decision required: No`, `Dependencies: —`, independently actionable). Both framings are individually accurate — they group `DEC-DATA-007` with the other three D1 decisions because all four gate the same `ENG-P2-001`/`ENG-P2-004` milestone (a sequencing relationship), not because they share subject matter or decision authority (a substantive relationship). Reported as a disclosed finding, not silently resolved in either direction — resolving it is a governance-ownership question left for a future task.
- **Dependency analysis performed:** `DEC-DATA-007` inherits two genuine constraints from the confirmed decisions — (1) from `DEC-PROV-004`, that loyalty-number/QR issuance happens only after canonical identity (Identity 1) resolution, per PRD2's pre-existing Registration Journey sequencing; (2) from `DEC-SEC-001`'s Identity Recovery Principles, that the loyalty number must never be reissued or regenerated during recovery, independently corroborated by the pre-existing AIR-003. No substantive relationship to `DEC-ID-003` was found — different actor category (business-side roles vs. customer identifiers) with no design-level interaction. No dependency beyond these two boundary conditions was found, corroborating the Register's own `Dependencies: —` field and `ENG-P2-000B`'s independent conclusion.
- **Scope boundary produced:** Already Resolved (loyalty-number permanence, non-reversibility, non-authenticating nature, QR content restrictions — all pre-existing PRD2/TRD12/AIR text, reinforced but not altered by the three confirmed decisions); Remaining Scope (concrete number format, QR payload scheme, generation-service invocation point); Out of Scope (any authentication, identity-recovery, trust-model, or permission-model change; any application code).
- **Repository consistency review:** no internal contradiction found among `DEC-DATA-007`'s own source documents (unlike the PRD1/PRD10 conflict `DEC-ID-003` resolved) — only the generation algorithm itself is undefined. Two pre-existing staleness items disclosed, not corrected: the Engineering Implementation Programme's Phase 2 "Blocking Reason" row still shows superseded statuses for `DEC-SEC-001`/`DEC-PROV-004`/`DEC-ID-003` (consistent with staleness already disclosed under `RES-004A` and its predecessors); the Requirements Traceability Matrix has no row referencing `DEC-DATA-007` at all, distinct from `DEC-ID-003`'s merely-stale `AP-008` row. No document updates were necessary to complete this analysis — the scope boundary was fully derivable from already-consistent source text.
- **Implementation prerequisites:** none inherited from `DEC-PROV-004` beyond the sequencing precondition; one inherited from `DEC-SEC-001` (generation must be idempotent per platform user, consistent with `ENG-P1-002`'s existing idempotency-service pattern); none inherited from `DEC-ID-003`. New, unique to `DEC-DATA-007`: collision-handling strategy, QR lookup/verification mechanism, and drafting the candidate algorithms/schemes the Register's own `Options identified` field currently leaves as "to be proposed."
- **Decision readiness:** Ready to prepare decision package — no unresolved repository conflict, no genuine cross-decision dependency, evidence-grounded scope boundary. One caveat disclosed: the scope-framing discrepancy (Founder-level vs. Engineering-Lead-owned framing) should be resolved explicitly before or during the next task.

## 2026-07-30 — RES-005 Pre-Merge Correction: Account for Existing Loyalty-Code Brief; Fix Scope Contradiction and Idempotency Wording

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Finding 1 (P2, confirmed genuine):** the analysis's original research pass missed `docs/00-governance/decisions/loyalty-code-decision-brief.md` — a pre-existing (2026-07-17), fully drafted decision-preparation document already proposing a loyalty-code format (`ABC-234`/`ABC-234-X`), a character-set/ambiguity analysis, an exact-calculation collision-probability table, and ten planning-level generation requirements including transactional uniqueness and retry-on-collision. §6, §7, and §8 corrected to cite this document and reframe the "new prerequisites" as evaluate-and-refine an existing proposal rather than draft-from-nothing; §3 and §1 lightly corrected for consistency.
- **Finding 2 (P2, confirmed genuine):** §5's "Remaining Scope" and "Out of Scope" lists directly contradicted each other on whether QR rotation/time-limiting is in scope — the former listed it as something `DEC-DATA-007` must resolve, the latter categorically excluded it because TRD12 §12.42 already defers it. Corrected by removing the item from "Remaining Scope"; TRD12's own deferral stands uncontested in "Out of Scope."
- **Finding 3 (P2, confirmed genuine):** §7's inherited-idempotency wording ("must be invoked exactly once... and never re-invoked") mischaracterized idempotency, which requires repeat-safe behavior, not single-invocation behavior — as written, it risked being carried into a future decision package as a rule that would reject legitimate retries (e.g., after a registration timeout) and strand registration. Corrected to the accurate invariant: at most one immutable assignment per platform user, with repeat calls returning the existing result.
- **Validation performed:** `npx prettier --check` on the corrected file, clean; `git status --short`/`git diff --stat` confirmed only the single intended analysis file changed.
- **Files modified:** `docs/00-governance/decisions/evidence/DEC-DATA-007-dependency-scope-analysis-2026-07-30.md`; `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this correction's own commit.
- **Report link:** [`docs/00-governance/decisions/evidence/DEC-DATA-007-dependency-scope-analysis-2026-07-30.md`](../00-governance/decisions/evidence/DEC-DATA-007-dependency-scope-analysis-2026-07-30.md).

## 2026-07-30 — RES-006: DEC-DATA-007 Engineering Decision Package

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Task:** prepare the engineering decision package for `DEC-DATA-007` ("Loyalty number and QR reference generation"), using `RES-005`'s completed Dependency & Scope Analysis as the authoritative scope boundary, per the Founder-authorized task "TASK — RES-006: DEC-DATA-007 Engineering Decision Package." Engineering decision preparation only — no identifier or QR generation implemented, no application code modified, no Founder review package prepared (none warranted — see below).
- **Pre-change analysis performed first (required by the brief):** checked `loyalty-code-decision-brief.md` (2026-07-17, pre-dating this Resolution Sprint) against all three now-confirmed decisions and found no conflict — the brief operates entirely on identifier-format/generation mechanics and never touches authentication, identity recovery, trust level, or business-role permissions. Identified what requires confirmation (the brief's core proposal has never been formally adopted), what requires refinement (idempotency framing; character-set selection), and what remains genuinely unresolved (QR payload: plain reference vs. signed token).
- **Recommendation:** formally adopt `loyalty-code-decision-brief.md`'s core proposal (format `ABC-234`/`ABC-234-X`, transactional uniqueness with retry-on-collision, immutable per-platform-user assignment) as drafted; resolve the QR-payload sub-choice in favor of a plain opaque reference (avoids introducing signing/key-management infrastructure the MVP's own constraints do not require); resolve the character-set sub-choice in favor of the brief's own recommendation (exclude `I`/`O` only, preserving codespace headroom).
- **Identifier and QR generation principles documented** (§9–§10 of the package): permanence, server-side-only generation, post-identity-resolution issuance, case-insensitive normalization, non-reuse on closure, audit logging (identifiers); reference-only QR payload, no personal data, rate-limited lookup, rotation explicitly out of scope per TRD12's own deferral (QR).
- **Collision/uniqueness analysis adopted from the brief without modification:** collision-probability table (≈0.07%–7.06% retry rate across MVP-to-regional-expansion scale) and transactional-uniqueness-plus-retry mechanism, both reused as-is from `loyalty-code-decision-brief.md` §5/§7.
- **Idempotency framing corrected and carried forward from `RES-005`'s own pre-merge correction:** at most one immutable assignment per platform user, with repeat calls returning the existing result — not "invoked exactly once."
- **Decision readiness: Ready for Engineering Lead decision** — no Founder package prepared, consistent with the Register's `Founder decision required: No` field, the Resolution Plan's Ownership Matrix, and the brief's own "recommended, not mandated" framing for Founder involvement; nothing in this analysis surfaces a constitutional or commercial-policy issue.
- **Validation performed:** confirmed all recommendations trace to `RES-005`'s scope boundary without contradiction; confirmed engineering recommendations are evidence-based (every claim cited to the brief, PRD2, TRD12, AIR, or the confirmed decisions); confirmed no constitutional issue introduced; confirmed the one genuinely unresolved sub-choice (QR payload) is presented as a recommendation, not an assumed decision; `npx prettier --check` clean; `git status --short`/`git diff --stat` confirmed only the intended two files changed.

## 2026-07-30 — RES-006A: DEC-DATA-007 Engineering Decision Recording

- **Date:** 2026-07-30
- **Task:** record the approved engineering decision for `DEC-DATA-007` ("Loyalty number and QR reference generation") in the live Decision Register, per the Founder task "TASK — RES-006A: DEC-DATA-007 Engineering Decision Recording." An Engineering Decision Recording task — no Founder decision, since `RES-006` found no constitutional or commercial issue; no application code modified; no identifier or QR format redesigned.

## 2026-07-31 — RES-005.2a: BaseMetadata Governing Blueprint Correction

- **Date:** 2026-07-31
- **Task:** merge PR #42, verify the resulting `main` state, then resolve the documentation-level BaseMetadata inconsistency between the Version 1 Engineering Blueprint §3.3 and TRD10 §10.5, establishing a single, deterministic contract for the future `RES-005.2b` code correction, per the Founder task "TASK — RES-005.2a: BaseMetadata Governing Blueprint Correction." Documentation-conformance task only — no application code modified, `EXT-TECH-001`/`DEC-PROD-012` not resolved, Phase 2 not authorized.
- **Stage A — PR #42 merged:** re-verified `OPEN`/`CLEAN`/`MERGEABLE`/CI-green, merged (merge commit `174983f4d559d54bc0d08c502c943844fbc5b7a4`), local `main` synchronized (`0/0` divergence, clean tree, no merge/rebase in progress), post-merge CI green (run `30614573004`), Phase 1 exit confirmed recorded `Approved`, Capability 2 confirmed still `Blocked` and not implementation-authorized.
- **Stage B — source-authority analysis:** confirmed TRD10 §10.5 is authoritative per the Blueprint's own §0 self-declared rule ("If this Blueprint and a TRD chapter ever appear to disagree, the TRD chapter governs and this document is corrected"), already established by `ENG-P2-000A` (2026-07-29) §5 and independently re-verified here by direct three-way comparison of TRD10 §10.5, Blueprint §3.3, and `baseMetadata.ts`. Identified the exact inconsistency as four distinct differences, not one: field naming (`version` vs `schemaVersion`, grounded in TRD10 §10.2's DAP-009); nullability (non-nullable vs `string | null` actor fields); field naming-and-semantics (`deletedAt`/`deletedBy` vs `archivedAt`/`archivedBy`, grounded in DAP-010 "Archive, Do Not Erase" and matching PRD2 §7's "Archived" account status, both independently verified); and field presence (`languageCode` — verified as a genuine, mandatory PRD2 §6 field, but domain-specific to `users`/`customerProfiles`, not part of TRD10's shared metadata shape — vs missing `currencyCode`/`timezone`, grounded in DAP-007).
- **New finding beyond `ENG-P2-000A`'s original scope, disclosed not corrected:** TRD8 §8.7 independently states the same non-conformant shape the Blueprint transcribed from it — a TRD-chapter-to-TRD-chapter inconsistency the Blueprint's own authority rule does not itself resolve (it only governs Blueprint-vs-TRD disagreements) and which correcting TRD8 itself is outside this task's authorization to fix.
- **Stage C — Blueprint corrected:** `docs/02-technical/version-1-engineering-blueprint.md` §3.3 rewritten to TRD10 §10.5's exact shape (base fields: `id`, `schemaVersion`, `status`, `createdAt`, `createdBy: string | null`, `updatedAt`, `updatedBy: string | null`; scoped fields: `businessId?`, `customerId?`, `countryCode?`, `currencyCode?`, `timezone?`, `archivedAt?: Timestamp | null`, `archivedBy?: string | null`; `languageCode` removed from the shared shape with its correct domain-specific home noted), with an inline correction note disclosing this task's own action, citing `ENG-P2-000A`, and disclosing the TRD8 divergence without correcting it. Front-matter updated.

## 2026-07-31 — RES-005.2b: BaseMetadata Code Conformance Correction

- **Date:** 2026-07-31
- **Task:** merge PR #44, verify the resulting `main` state, perform a mandatory implementation impact analysis, then correct `functions/src/shared/metadata/baseMetadata.ts` into full conformance with the now-aligned documentation baseline (TRD10 §10.5, the corrected Engineering Blueprint §3.3, the reconciled TRD8 §8.7, and the approved `RES-005.2a` Contract Analysis), per the Founder task "TASK — RES-005.2b: BaseMetadata Code Conformance Correction." The first code-level change in this task chain — no other application code touched, `EXT-TECH-001`/`DEC-PROD-012` not resolved, Phase 2 not authorized, the approved contract itself not altered.
- **Stage A — PR #44 merged:** re-verified `OPEN`/`CLEAN`/`MERGEABLE`/CI-green, merged (merge commit `e931112f08b1753f314af30bdc0fccc9737323dc`), local `main` synchronized (`0/0` divergence, clean tree, no merge/rebase in progress). Confirmed live: TRD10 §10.5 unchanged/authoritative, Blueprint §3.3 aligned, TRD8 §8.7 reconciled, `baseMetadata.ts` still non-conformant (all four discrepancies unchanged), Capability 2 still `Blocked`.
- **Stage B — mandatory implementation impact analysis:** repository-wide search confirmed **zero consumers** of `BaseMetadata`/`stampCreate`/`stampUpdate` anywhere outside the module itself — no domain code exists yet in `functions/src` (only shared infrastructure: commands, correlation, errors, events, idempotency, logging, metadata, outbox, validation). Confirmed no Firestore converter, persistence adapter, API type, fixture, factory, mock, or seed data references the type; confirmed `firestore.rules`/`storage.rules` are blanket deny-by-default with zero field-level logic naming any BaseMetadata field; confirmed the Requirements Traceability Matrix has no BaseMetadata reference (checked directly, not assumed). No material ambiguity was found in the approved contract — Stage B did not trigger a stop-and-report. The full 14-point Pre-edit Impact Statement is recorded in the accompanying Code Conformance Report §5.
- **Stage C — implementation:** corrected `functions/src/shared/metadata/baseMetadata.ts` field-for-field against the approved contract: renamed `version`→`schemaVersion`; widened `createdBy`/`updatedBy` from non-nullable `string` to `string | null`; renamed `deletedAt?`/`deletedBy?`→`archivedAt?`/`archivedBy?`; removed the incorrectly-present `languageCode?`; added the missing `currencyCode?`/`timezone?`; added `readonly` to `id`, `createdAt`, `createdBy` per the contract's explicit immutability classification. `stampCreate`/`stampUpdate` function bodies required no change — both already derived timestamps exclusively from `serverTimestamp()` (DAP-008) and passed the actor through untransformed, already satisfying the corrected nullable signature. No convenience field, alias, or client-controlled audit metadata was introduced.
- **Stage D — compatibility and migration assessment: No migration required.** `.firebaserc` has no `production` alias (only `dev`/`staging`); no seed, fixture, or migration-utility directory exists; zero consumers means no domain code has ever written a document through this module in any environment — read/write/rollback compatibility are all trivially satisfied because no data of the old or any shape exists to migrate. No compatibility reader, backfill, or formal migration was implemented; no historical data was transformed.
- **Stage E — tests and validation:** added two runtime tests (`stampCreate(null)`, `stampUpdate(null)`) proving the now-explicit nullable-actor contract; four pre-existing tests retained unchanged (6/6 total in `baseMetadata.test.ts`). Full local CI-equivalent suite run: `pnpm --filter functions typecheck`/`lint`/`build` clean; **94/94** `functions` unit tests pass (no regression); root-level `pnpm build`/`lint`/`format:check`/`typecheck`/`test` clean, **191/191** `apps/web` tests pass (unaffected); real Firebase Emulator Suite (`pnpm emulators:validate`) **23/23** tests pass, no flake on this run. A genuine testing-methodology finding was discovered and disclosed rather than worked around: `functions/tsconfig.json` excludes `.test.ts` files from `tsc --noEmit` (the command CI's Typecheck step actually runs), so TypeScript type-only assertions inside test files (e.g. `@ts-expect-error`) provide zero real enforcement in this repository — empirically confirmed by running such assertions against the unmodified, non-conformant source and observing all pass. Type-shape conformance is instead proven via the field-level Conformance Matrix plus `pnpm typecheck` passing against the corrected source file itself, matching how CI actually validates types. `functions/tsconfig.json`/`vitest.config.ts` were inspected but deliberately not modified — an out-of-scope tooling change, not required by this bounded correction.
- **Stage F — security and data-integrity review:** confirmed the module remains server-only with no client-reachable import path; `stampCreate`/`stampUpdate` derive all timestamps exclusively from `serverTimestamp()`, so no client-supplied timestamp is possible; the widened `string | null` actor type introduces no new forgery surface (no authorization decision was or is made by this module); `firestore.rules`/`storage.rules` are unaffected (no field-level Rule referenced any BaseMetadata field name before or after); no internal identifier newly exposed; no server-side validation existed to bypass. No directly-required security control was found missing.
- **Stage G — traceability and programme synchronisation:** updated the Engineering Implementation Programme (Phase 1/Phase 2 summary rows, Phase 2 Current Status, Work-Packages Blocking Reason — recording BaseMetadata conformance as fully resolved while explicitly preserving Capability 2's `Blocked` status and `EXT-TECH-001`/`DEC-PROD-012` as unchanged), the 11thONUS Master Workflow (two locations), `CDR-001`, and the Coding-Agent Prompt Register — each corrected only on the specific "code conformance still pending (`RES-005.2b`)" claim now factually false, following the same narrow-scoping discipline applied throughout this task chain. Confirmed the Requirements Traceability Matrix still has no BaseMetadata reference requiring correction. No historical report was rewritten.
- **Stage H — blocker reassessment: Resolved.** Documentation conformance Complete (`RES-005.2a`/`RES-005.2a-R1`); code conformance Complete (`RES-005.2b`, this task); test conformance Complete (6/6 + 94/94 + 191/191 + 23/23, all passing); security-rule conformance N/A/Unaffected (no field-level Rule logic exists for this type); data migration status No migration required; operational readiness N/A pending a future domain consumer. **Capability 2 remains `Blocked`** — Gate item 7 is now Satisfied; Gate items 1 (`EXT-TECH-001`) and 6 (`DEC-PROD-012`) remain open and unchanged. Resolving BaseMetadata does not, by itself, authorize Phase 2 feature work.
- **Deliverables produced:** the Implementation Impact Analysis, the BaseMetadata Code Correction, the field-level Conformance Matrix (all 15 fields, including `languageCode`'s removal), the Compatibility and Migration Assessment, Test Evidence, the Security and Integrity Assessment, the Capability Blocker Reassessment, and the identified-but-not-executed Next-Step Recommendation (`EXT-TECH-001-EVIDENCE`) — all in the accompanying Code Conformance Report.
- **Validation performed:** live re-verification of PR #44's state before merge and of `main`'s state after; repository-wide `grep` confirming zero BaseMetadata consumers and zero prior `@ts-expect-error` usage; full local CI-equivalent suite (typecheck/lint/build/format/unit/emulator) run and passing as itemized in Stage E; confirmed no application code outside the two intended `functions/src/shared/metadata/` files was touched; confirmed no decision recorded or reopened, no Phase 2 feature work begun, no approved contract field altered; `npx prettier --check` clean; `git status --short`/`git diff --stat` confirmed only the intended files changed.
- **Files created:** `docs/05-implementation/reports/RES-005.2b-basemetadata-code-conformance-report-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Files modified:** `functions/src/shared/metadata/baseMetadata.ts`; `functions/src/shared/metadata/baseMetadata.test.ts`; `docs/05-implementation/change-tracking/engineering-implementation-programme.md`; `docs/05-implementation/11thonus-master-workflow.md`; `docs/05-implementation/roadmap/CDR-001-capability-delivery-roadmap.md`; `docs/05-implementation/change-tracking/coding-agent-prompt-register.md`; `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md`.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none introduced — a server-only module with zero live consumers and zero persisted data, the widest possible safety margin for a type-shape correction. No Firebase Rule, persistence adapter, or client-reachable code path touched. The disclosed testing-methodology gap is a pre-existing repository-tooling characteristic, not a risk newly introduced by this task, and was not silently worked around by modifying `tsconfig.json`/`vitest.config.ts`.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's own commit — trivially safe, no consumer imports this module and no persisted data exists in the old or new shape.

- **Stage B/C — governance evidence analysis and consistency matrix:** reviewed the Resolution Plan (`ENG-P2-RES-000` §7), the External Dependencies Register, the Decision Register's full `DEC-SEC-001`/`DEC-PROV-004` entries, `ENG-P1-EXIT-001`'s Phase 1 Exit Determination Report, the Engineering Implementation Programme, the Master Workflow, `CDR-001`, and the Coding-Agent Prompt Register. **Finding: no genuine conflict exists.** Both Founder decisions scope their own disclaimer narrowly and explicitly — `DEC-SEC-001`: "not a blocker to this decision"; `DEC-PROV-004` Final Decision Principle 8: "a production-readiness condition rather than a governance blocker" — neither purports to amend the separate Resolution Plan's own Capability Authorisation Gate (§7 item 1), which governs a different milestone (implementation start, not decision closure) and remains textually unmodified since 2026-07-29. `ENG-P1-EXIT-001` (written after both decisions were `CONFIRMED`) already independently reconciled this, recording both classifications side by side without contradiction. ## 2026-07-31 — EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS: Controlled Real-SMS Test Harness

- **Date:** 2026-07-31
- **Task:** merge PR #49, verify the resulting `main` state, then build a minimal, development-only test harness capable of invoking the genuine Firebase Authentication Phone Sign-In SMS route, per the Founder task "FOUNDER AUTHORISATION AND TASK — EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS: Controlled Real-SMS Test Harness." A bounded harness-preparation task — no real SMS sent, no real phone number stored or committed, no additional authentication provider enabled, no Customer Identity implementation begun.
- **Stage A — PR #49 merged:** re-verified `OPEN`/`CLEAN`/`MERGEABLE`/CI-green, no unresolved review threads, merged (merge commit `597763e7d59c0dd16a46be102b1e37a067aa8047`), local `main` synchronized (`0/0` divergence, clean tree), post-merge CI green (run `30646494584`).
- **Pre-edit analysis:** found the shared `infrastructure/firebase/auth.ts` Auth instance auto-connects to the Auth Emulator whenever `import.meta.env.DEV` is true — the same flag gating this harness — meaning reusing it would silently route "real" test sends to the emulator. Resolved by giving the harness its own, distinctly-named secondary Firebase app, never emulator-connected.
- **Implementation, test-driven throughout:** four new modules under `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/` — `mask.ts` (phone-number masking), `harnessGate.ts` (explicit-boolean dev-only gate, mirroring `config/env.ts`'s existing `viteFlags` pattern), `phoneAuthHarnessAuth.ts` (dedicated, never-emulator-connected Auth instance; refuses to activate against the Emulator Suite's demo-project config), and `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx` (the harness UI — runtime-only phone/OTP input, `RecaptchaVerifier` + `signInWithPhoneNumber` + `ConfirmationResult.confirm`, manual "Mark SMS Received" button, masked results display, full reset). Wired at a new dev-only route (`/dev/phone-auth-harness`) in `App.tsx`.
- **Privacy controls, each independently verified by an automated test:** no pre-populated number; masking after submission; no `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` writes; no number/OTP in the URL; OTP never logged (console-spy verified); OTP and number cleared on reset and on successful verification; error display limited to Firebase's `.code` only, never `.message`; "SMS received" set only by a manual tester click, never automatically. The harness imports nothing from `observability/*` and its dedicated Auth instance is never wired into `registerAuthLifecycle`, so no harness auth-state event reaches the diagnostics pipeline.
- **Development-only exclusion — a genuine defect found and corrected before completion, disclosed transparently:** the first implementation guarded the route with a plain `&&`-conditional JSX branch; a real `pnpm build` followed by grepping `dist/` for harness markers found the harness **was** present in the production bundle despite the runtime guard correctly rendering nothing. Corrected by gating a `React.lazy()` dynamic import directly on the literal `import.meta.env.DEV` (not through an intermediate function), which Vite's production build statically eliminates. Re-verified: a fresh `pnpm build` followed by the same grep found zero harness markers in `dist/`. A residual `signInWithPhoneNumber`/`RecaptchaVerifier` string match was investigated and traced to Firebase Auth SDK's own pre-existing internal REST/reCAPTCHA machinery (already bundled because the main app imports `firebase/auth` for unrelated reasons since `ENG-P1-003-IMP-02`), not to the harness.
- **Test evidence:** `mask.test.ts` 4/4, `harnessGate.test.ts` 3/3, `phoneAuthHarnessAuth.test.ts` 5/5, `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx` 16/16 (Firebase fully mocked — no automated test ever calls real Firebase or sends a real SMS), `App.test.tsx` 2/2. Full `apps/web` suite **220/220** (191 pre-existing + 29 new); `functions` suite **94/94** unaffected. `pnpm typecheck`/`lint`/`format:check`/`build` all clean (two genuine defects — TypeScript spread-argument errors in mock setup, one unused-variable lint finding — caught and fixed by these exact commands, not asserted away). Real Firebase Emulator Suite (`pnpm emulators:validate`): **23/23** pass, no flake.
- **Deliverables:** the harness implementation itself; a Founder-operated [Manual Runbook](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-manual-runbook-2026-07-31.md) (13 numbered steps, explicit instruction never to paste real numbers/OTPs into any coding-agent conversation or repository file); a privacy-safe [Evidence Template](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-delivery-test-evidence-template-2026-07-31.md) with separately-labelled technical-proof and launch-reliability thresholds (both stated as engineering recommendations, since no governing document defines either).
- **Post-push CI correction — a second genuine defect found and corrected before completion, disclosed transparently:** after PR #50 was opened, GitHub Actions run `30648447370` failed on `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx`'s "timing evidence" test — CI's Node runtime exposed a test race condition that had passed reliably in every local run. Root cause: six occurrences of `await screen.findByText(/request accepted/i);` used an unanchored regex that matched the harness's synchronous "Request accepted: No" render just as readily as the desired "Yes" state, letting the assertion proceed before the mocked async `signInWithPhoneNumber` call had actually resolved. Fixed by replacing all six with `await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /mark.*received/i });`, which only resolves once `requestAccepted` is genuinely `true`. Re-verified locally: full `apps/web` suite **220/220** pass; `pnpm typecheck`/`pnpm lint`/`pnpm format:check` all clean. Only the test file changed — no production source was touched, so the §9 build-exclusion finding is unaffected.
- **`EXT-TECH-001` status: unchanged, Still Pending** — this task does not, and is not authorised to, resolve it; the harness's existence is preparation, not delivery evidence. **Capability 2 remains `Blocked`** on `EXT-TECH-001`/`DEC-PROD-012` only. Google Sign-In and every other authentication provider remain disabled — confirmed unchanged.
- **Validation performed:** live re-verification of PR #49's state before merge and of `main`'s state after; a full pre-edit codebase analysis before any file was created; TDD applied to every new module (each test written and confirmed failing before implementation); a real production build performed twice, with `dist/` re-inspected both times to genuinely verify — not merely assert — the harness's exclusion; confirmed no phone number or OTP appears in any tracked file; confirmed no automated test or manual action sent a real SMS; `git status --short`/`git diff --stat` confirmed only the intended files changed.
- **Files created:** `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/{mask,harnessGate,phoneAuthHarnessAuth}.ts` + their `.test.ts` files; `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx` + `.test.tsx`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-manual-runbook-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-delivery-test-evidence-template-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Files modified:** `apps/web/src/App.tsx` (one new, build-time-excluded, dev-only route); `apps/web/src/App.test.tsx` (one new test); `docs/00-governance/documentation-changes-log.md` (Entry 049, append-only).
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none — no `.env.example`, `firebase.json`, `.firebaserc`, or live Firebase project configuration was changed.
- **Risks:** none introduced beyond the tool's inherent, disclosed, intentional capability (sending a real billable SMS once a human populates real credentials and manually clicks Send) — gated by requiring manual local secret population, a manual click, and being unreachable in any deployed build. Two genuine implementation defects this task introduced — incomplete production exclusion, and a test race condition — were each caught (by this task's own build-verification step, and by CI, respectively) and corrected before completion; neither was left latent.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's own commit removes all new files and both edits in one action. Harness-only removal: delete `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/` and its route registration in `App.tsx`. PR #49's merge is independently reversible per its own disclosed rollback instructions, requiring fresh Founder authorization, out of scope here.
- **Report link:** [`docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md`](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md).

## 2026-08-01 — EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-CR1: Corrective Review Cycle for PR #50

- **Task:** the Founder reviewed PR #50, accepted a live merge-readiness check, found 3 unresolved automated-review findings on the harness's own source, and declined to merge — issuing `EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-CR1` to fix all three before the PR could return for merge authorisation. No merge occurred.
- **Finding 1 (P1), fixed — environment safety:** `phoneAuthHarnessAuth.ts` rejected only the Emulator Suite's demo project; any other real project (staging, production, unknown) passed the guard silently. Replaced the negative-only check with a positive allowlist against a new `APPROVED_DEV_PROJECT_ID = "eleventh-on-us-dev"` constant (mirroring `functions/src/config/region.ts`'s `PLATFORM_REGION` pattern), not runtime-configurable. Fails closed for the demo project, staging, production, any unknown project, and a missing project ID alike.
- **Finding 2 (P1), fixed — evidence accuracy:** the displayed "Delivery latency" measured from Firebase's acceptance of the request, not from the Send click, silently excluding reCAPTCHA/network time from the core deliverable metric. Corrected to measure Send click → tester-confirmed receipt; the original acceptance-based figure is preserved as a second, explicitly-labelled internal-diagnostic line.
- **Finding 3 (P2), fixed — retry validation:** the Send button existed only pre-submission, so the dead retry-increment branch could never execute — "Retry count" could never exceed 0. Added a bounded (`MAX_RETRY_COUNT = 3`) Retry/Resend control, always visible after a request, that resends to the same masked identity/carrier, clears stale OTP/result/timing state, and genuinely increments the count; disables once the bound is reached.
- **Test-design defect found and fixed mid-cycle, disclosed transparently:** the first draft of the new timing tests mocked `Date.now()` directly, which collided with Testing Library's own internal `Date.now()` use inside `findByRole`'s polling — caught by watching the tests fail for an unexpected reason (not the expected one) before accepting RED. Rewrote both using real timers with a deliberate delay and a numeric-threshold assertion instead.
- **Local worktree anomaly, disclosed transparently:** the worktree's `.git` link file was found missing before this cycle's edits began. `git worktree list` (from the primary checkout, read-only) and direct inspection of the main repo's `.git/worktrees/eng-p1-003-imp-05/` confirmed the worktree's registration and admin metadata were fully intact; the pointer file was restored with its standard, single-line content (the exact content `git worktree add` itself writes). `git checkout -- .` then restored several hundred tracked files verbatim from the object database (confirmed via `git diff --stat HEAD` showing pure deletions, zero content divergence, before the restore) — no source content was ever reconstructed from memory. `pnpm install` separately relinked broken `node_modules` symlinks from the intact `.pnpm` store. The branch, its commits, and PR #50's CI evidence on GitHub were independently re-verified before and after and were never affected. See the implementation report's §30.5 for full detail.
- **Test evidence:** `phoneAuthHarnessAuth.test.ts` 5 → **11/11**; `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx` 16 → **27/27**. Full `apps/web` suite **237/237** pass (up from 220). `pnpm typecheck`/`lint`/`format:check`/`build` all clean; production-bundle exclusion re-verified via `grep dist/` — zero matches, including for the new Retry/Resend control.
- **Validation performed:** confirmed live — only `eleventh-on-us-dev` can activate the harness; latency is measured from Send click; retry count can exceed zero and is bounded/disables at 3; no real phone number/OTP appears anywhere in the diff; no real SMS was sent (all Firebase calls remain mocked); Google Sign-In and all other providers remain unchanged; `EXT-TECH-001` remains `Still Pending`; Capability 2 remains `Blocked`.
- **Files modified:** `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/{phoneAuthHarnessAuth.ts,phoneAuthHarnessAuth.test.ts,PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx,PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx}`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-manual-runbook-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-delivery-test-evidence-template-2026-07-31.md`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md` (§30 addendum); `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none introduced beyond the harness's already-disclosed capability. `MAX_RETRY_COUNT = 3` is a deliberate, conservative bound for a manually-operated test tool, documented as such in code and the runbook.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this cycle's commit restores the pre-CR1 harness (with its three now-disclosed defects), independent of the original harness commit and the earlier CI-fix commit.
- **Post-push CI flake (unrelated), disclosed transparently:** CI run `30691479650` on `74aeba6` initially failed on two `functions/` emulator-concurrency tests — a directory this cycle never touched. A no-code-change rerun (`gh run rerun --failed`) turned it fully green, confirming the same pre-existing flakiness category disclosed earlier in this task chain (ENG-P1-002's TR record), not a regression.
- **Review threads:** replies citing the fix commit were posted to all three findings, which were then explicitly resolved via GitHub's `resolveReviewThread` mutation. **0 unresolved review threads remain.**

## 2026-08-01 — EXT-TECH-001-HARNESS-CR2: Post-Merge Pre-Send Failure Resolution

- **Context:** PR #50 was subsequently Founder-authorised and merged to `main` at `3d4206a57fc3af742bceaa8ac0de7cfd515bfd7b` (post-merge CI green). A live-test-preparation task then registered the harness's own placeholder Firebase Web App (none existed under `eleventh-on-us-dev`) and fixed local-only `.env.local` runtime-error findings. The Founder's own subsequent live-test attempt failed with `auth/invalid-app-credential` on first Send, then `"reCAPTCHA has already been rendered in this element"` on Retry — `EXT-TECH-001-HARNESS-CR2` required investigating and resolving both without guessing, without a further real SMS attempt, and without changing production authentication behaviour.
- **New evidence mid-task:** the Founder manually registered the project's official Web App (`"11thONUS Web - Development"`, App ID `1:709450867178:web:1195c6a790be6ee7a99293`) — distinct from this task chain's own earlier auto-created placeholder app. Re-fetched, explicitly `--project`-scoped (never relying on the CLI's ambient "active project," confirmed to be a *different* project, `eleventh-on-us`): confirmed the new app exists, retrieved its official SDK config, and confirmed `apiKey`/`authDomain`/`projectId`/`storageBucket`/`messagingSenderId` are identical to the placeholder app's (project-level defaults, not per-app) — only `appId` differs. `.env.local` updated to the Founder's official App ID; dev server restarted (Vite does not hot-reload env files).
- **Root cause 1 — `auth/invalid-app-credential`:** ruled out with direct evidence, not assumption — reCAPTCHA Enterprise is confirmed *not* enforced for phone auth on this project (live `recaptchaConfig` query); billing is confirmed enabled (Blaze); domain/SMS-region/Phone-Auth-enabled were already confirmed correct. A reCAPTCHA token was successfully generated in isolation (a `RecaptchaVerifier.verify()` call made directly against the harness's own dedicated Auth instance, never calling `signInWithPhoneNumber`, and therefore incapable of sending an SMS) — proving the token-generation layer is healthy against the current, Founder-registered configuration. **Category: Firebase configuration (most likely cause, unconfirmed)** — the total absence of any Founder-registered Web App at the time of the original failure is a real, now-closed gap, but not a confirmed cause: the placeholder app's `apiKey`/`projectId` (the fields Identity Toolkit actually validates) were already identical to the Founder's official app, so only `appId` — a field Identity Toolkit's phone-auth check does not use — differed. This could not be 100%-proven without repeating the original failing call, which this task explicitly prohibits; treat this as an open hypothesis pending a new Founder-operated live request, not a resolved root cause.
- **Root cause 2 — reCAPTCHA "already rendered" on retry, fixed:** confirmed directly via DOM inspection (a container reused across two `.verify()` calls permanently retains the first call's rendered `grecaptcha` widget) and validated against Firebase's own official Phone Auth documentation, which explicitly instructs resetting the reCAPTCHA before any retry after a failed `signInWithPhoneNumber` call. CR1's retry design reused the same `RecaptchaVerifier` instance across retries without ever clearing it — a genuine RecaptchaVerifier-lifecycle code defect. **Fixed** in `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx`'s `performSend`: every send attempt (first send and every retry alike) now unconditionally clears any existing verifier and constructs a fresh one, instead of only constructing one when none exists yet. A first send's behaviour is unchanged (the clear is a safe no-op when nothing exists yet); only retry behaviour changes, exactly where the defect lived.
- **Test evidence:** test-first — added a regression test proving the pre-fix code passed the *same* verifier instance to `signInWithPhoneNumber` across a send and a retry (referential-equality assertion), confirmed it failed for exactly that reason, then applied the fix. `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx` 27 → **28/28**. Full `apps/web` suite **238/238** pass (up from 237). A genuine `TS2493` tuple-indexing TypeScript error surfaced by the new test (the mock's inferred call-argument type has no declared parameters) was fixed via an explicit cast, not by weakening any production type.
- **Validation performed:** `pnpm typecheck`/`lint`/`format:check`/`build` all clean; production-bundle exclusion re-verified via `grep dist/` — zero matches; secret/PII scan on the diff clean; `git diff --stat 3d4206a` confirms only `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx` and its test file changed — the two files directly responsible, per this task's explicit scope instruction. No further real SMS was sent at any point — every diagnostic either queried public/read-only Firebase metadata or exercised `RecaptchaVerifier.verify()` alone, which cannot itself reach Identity Toolkit's phone endpoint.
- **Files modified:** `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/{PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx,PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx}`; `docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md` (§31 addendum); `docs/changes/IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGES.md` (this entry).
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none to any Firebase project setting (the Web App registration was the Founder's own action, not this task's); local-only `.env.local` (never committed) updated to the Founder's official App ID.
- **Risks:** none. This is a strictly additive lifecycle fix to a development-only tool; no production authentication code path was touched.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this cycle's commit restores the CR1 retry behaviour (reused verifier across retries, reproducing the "already rendered" defect on the next retry after any failure).
- **`EXT-TECH-001` status: unchanged, Still Pending. Capability 2: unchanged, `Blocked`.** The harness has not been re-tested end-to-end with a real send since this fix — that remains the Founder's own next live-test step.
- **Report link:** [`docs/05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md`](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md) (§31).

## 2026-08-01 — EXT-TECH-001-HARNESS-CR3: Firebase Hosting Preview and Hosted-Domain Phone Authentication Correction

- **Context:** the Founder enabled standard Firebase Hosting on `eleventh-on-us-dev` (site created, no release deployed) and authorised completing repository-side Hosting setup, building a tightly-scoped harness-only artifact, deploying it to a temporary Hosting preview channel, authorising only that exact hostname for Firebase Auth, fixing the deeper reCAPTCHA lifecycle defect the Founder found still present after CR2, and preparing the HTTPS preview for a Founder-operated real-SMS test. No real SMS was sent at any point.
- **Harness isolation approach:** a dedicated hosted-preview build entry (`apps/web/harness.html` + `harnessMain.tsx`), not a guarded route on the existing app bundle — the app's shared composition root (`main.tsx`) pulls in Firebase App Check (fails closed outside dev without a site key), the full observability pipeline, and `react-query`, none of which this harness may touch. `vite.config.ts` swaps the build entry and omits the PWA plugin only when `mode === "test-harness"`; the ordinary build is untouched. Empirically confirmed: 32 modules transformed vs. 2,223 for the full app; the compiled harness bundle contains zero occurrences of `react-router`, `QueryClient`, `Sentry`, `ObservabilityErrorBoundary`, `registerAuthLifecycle`, or `initializeFirebasePlatform`.
- **CSP:** derived from real, observed network traffic (an isolated, SMS-safe `RecaptchaVerifier.verify()` call revealed the exact domains contacted), not assumption — `script-src`/`frame-src`: `www.google.com`, `www.gstatic.com`; `connect-src` additionally: `identitytoolkit.googleapis.com`, `securetoken.googleapis.com`. `frame-ancestors` deliberately untouched per explicit Founder instruction — Google's own report-only `frame-ancestors` warning on `recaptcha.google.com`/`www.google.com` responses describes who may frame *their* content, not ours.
- **Test-build boundary:** new `VITE_ENABLE_PHONE_AUTH_TEST_HARNESS` flag, combined with the Vite mode and resolved project ID in a new pure, fail-closed gate (`testHarnessGate.ts`) — all three conditions must match exactly. `PhoneAuthHarnessPage` re-checks this at runtime via a new `testHarnessBuild` prop (defaulting to `false`), independent of the build-entry swap, so a hosted build's own component still refuses to render if the conditions don't hold.
- **Root cause 2 — reCAPTCHA "already rendered," post-CR2:** reproduced directly — `.clear()` resets the widget's internal state but does **not** remove the DOM nodes `grecaptcha.render()` injected into the container; a new `RecaptchaVerifier` against that same, still-populated container throws on its next `.verify()` regardless of being freshly constructed. **Fixed** by rendering into a genuinely fresh, never-before-used DOM node per attempt (`recaptchaWrapperRef` + `createFreshRecaptchaContainer()`/`teardownRecaptchaVerifier()`), applied on every send, retry, reset, and unmount, plus a synchronous `isSendingRef` guard against overlapping sends. Verified against the real widget (two sequential fresh-container `.verify()` calls both succeeded) before any test was written.
- **Test evidence:** test-first throughout. `testHarnessGate.test.ts`: 10/10. `PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx`: 3 new Stage-D tests + 8 new Stage-E regression tests (fresh container per attempt, prior container removed, recovery after failure, pristine container on reset-after-failure, no accumulation across repeated resets, unmount cleanup, exactly one verifier per Send under StrictMode, blocked overlapping retry) — 39/39 in the file. Full `apps/web` suite: **259/259** (up from 238). `functions` suite unaffected: 94/94. Playwright e2e smoke: 1/1.
- **Official Firebase config re-verification:** re-fetched fresh, compared programmatically (Yes/No only, no raw values recorded) against `.env.test-harness.local` (gitignored) — all 6 fields (apiKey, authDomain, projectId, storageBucket, messagingSenderId, appId) match.
- **Deployment:** `firebase hosting:channel:deploy phone-auth-test --project eleventh-on-us-dev --expires 6h`. Channel `phone-auth-test`, URL `https://eleventh-on-us-dev--phone-auth-test-3yz68r9z.web.app`, expiry `2026-08-01 23:46:31`. Live channel confirmed untouched before and after (still no release, genuine "Site Not Found").
- **Auth authorized domain:** the exact preview hostname was found already present in `authorizedDomains` — Firebase's own Hosting-Auth integration auto-adds a preview channel's hostname on creation; confirmed genuinely new (absent pre-deploy). No wildcard, no other domain touched, no manual write performed.
- **Preflight finding, fixed:** a `firebase.json` header rule scoped to `/index.html` literally did not apply to `/` (the path browsers actually request) — confirmed via `curl -I` on both paths. Added an explicit `"/"` rule, redeployed to the same channel, re-verified via `curl -I` and a live-channel re-check.
- **Hosted preflight:** HTTPS, page loads, prominent test-only banner, harness guard passes, single-page deploy (no other routes), console completely empty (zero errors, zero CSP violations), no phone number/OTP present, `signInWithPhoneNumber` never called. `script-src`'s Google-domain allowance verified live (real `recaptcha/api.js` loaded, zero CSP violations). Disclosed limitation: the full challenge-iframe round trip was validated on `localhost` against the same domains, not literally re-executed on this exact URL.
- **Files modified:** `firebase.json` (hosting only); `apps/web/vite.config.ts`; `apps/web/harness.html` (new); `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/{harnessMain.tsx,testHarnessGate.ts,testHarnessGate.test.ts}` (new); `apps/web/src/dev/phoneAuthHarness/{PhoneAuthHarnessPage.tsx,PhoneAuthHarnessPage.test.tsx}`; `apps/web/.env.example`; `apps/web/.env.test-harness.local` (new, gitignored, never committed). No unrelated file touched; Firestore/Storage/Functions/Emulator config untouched; `App.tsx` and the dev-server harness route unchanged.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** `firebase.json`'s `hosting` block gained `rewrites`/`headers`; no Firebase *project* setting changed (Hosting itself was already enabled by the Founder).
- **Risks:** none to production — untouched composition root, no live-channel deploy. The narrow CSP would need review before any future full-application production Hosting deploy reuses this `firebase.json` (fails loudly with visible violations if reused as-is, not silently).
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this cycle's commit(s) restores the pre-CR3 state. The deployed preview channel is independent of git history and is torn down per the teardown procedure regardless.
- **`EXT-TECH-001` status: unchanged, Still Pending. Capability 2: unchanged, `Blocked`.** Environment preparation: complete. Real carrier test: not yet performed — remains the Founder's own next step via the preview URL above.
- **Report links:** [`EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md`](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-TEST-HARNESS-implementation-report-2026-07-31.md) (§32); [`EXT-TECH-001-HARNESS-CR3-hosted-preview-record.md`](../05-implementation/reports/EXT-TECH-001-HARNESS-CR3-hosted-preview-record.md) (persistent state record).

## 2026-08-01 — IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001: Progressive Trust Constitutional Realignment (governance analysis only, no code change)

- **Context:** the Founder issued `FD-IDENTITY-001` ("Progressive Trust Identity Strategy"), a constitutional decision separating Authentication, Identity, and Verification into independent capabilities and removing mandatory phone verification from initial loyalty-programme participation. Task `IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001` required a complete repository impact assessment and Founder-approval-document preparation **before any engineering resumes** — the task brief explicitly prohibited modifying application code.
- **Critical finding:** no engineering implementation exists yet against the pre-`FD-IDENTITY-001` model — `ENG-P2-001` (Customer Identity) remains `Blocked` with zero code. This decision costs nothing in rework, only in documentation/decision-register correction, because it arrives before the capability it redefines was built.
- **Tension identified:** `DEC-PROV-004` (CONFIRMED 2026-07-30) declares "the verified mobile phone number is the customer's canonical identity" — the exact conflation `FD-IDENTITY-001` corrects. `DEC-SEC-001` (CONFIRMED 2026-07-30) names "reward redemption" as a capability phone verification may gate — directly contradicted by `FD-IDENTITY-001`'s explicit inclusion of standard-reward redemption among the capabilities available without mandatory verification.
- **Amendment, not supersession, recommended** for both `DEC-PROV-004` and `DEC-SEC-001` — targeted clause-level corrections only; provider selections, recovery mechanics, and the general progressive-trust shape remain correct and load-bearing. `DEC-ID-003` requires no substantive change (citation-currency only, once `DEC-PROV-004` is amended). `DEC-DATA-007`/`DEC-ID-001` confirmed unaffected.
- **Constitutional grounding already present:** Platform Constitution CP-007 ("Progressive KYC... Registration should remain lightweight") already aligns with `FD-IDENTITY-001` — cited as prior support, not introduced as new philosophy.
- **Recommended capability split:** the current, conflated "Capability 2 — Customer Identity" (bundling auth + profile + loyalty-number/QR into one work package) splits into three: a narrowed Capability 2 (Identity — permanent ID/loyalty-number/QR/profile), a new Authentication capability (provider-agnostic access), and a new **Identity Trust Management (ITM)** capability — the Founder's own recommended internal engineering name for the Progressive Trust principle, adopted throughout. "Progressive Trust" remains the correct name when quoting the Founder's constitutional principle; ITM is internal/engineering-only, never customer-facing.
- **8-step migration sequence recommended:** (1) Founder countersign; (2) Decision Register update (`DEC-PROV-004`/`DEC-SEC-001` amendment); (3) governing-document correction pass (PRD2 §5/§7, TRD12 §12.3/§12.4.1, Canonical Reference §10); (4) `CDR-001` capability restructuring; (5) Capability Authorisation Gate re-scoping; (6) RTM/Programme sync; (7) trust-signal schema design (TRD10 `users` collection currently has no `phoneVerified`/trust-level field); (8) implementation begins. Full detail in the linked reports.
- **Documents affected (read-only analysis, none modified):** Decision Register (`DEC-PROV-004`, `DEC-SEC-001`, `DEC-ID-003`); `CDR-001` §5; Capability 2 Resolution Plan §7; Canonical Reference §10/§4; PRD2 §4/§5/§7; TRD12 §12.3/§12.4.1/§12.30-31; TRD10 §10.6.1; RTM (~48 rows, all "Not Started," no completed work to correct); Engineering Implementation Programme Phase 2. **Confirmed unaffected:** `verified-loyalty-principles.md`/governance-freeze doc (Reward Lifecycle Engine only, explicitly out of identity scope) — a pre-existing, unrelated finding disclosed in passing: neither file is committed to git history despite being cited as constitutional authority by several Decision Register entries; flagged, not fixed (out of scope for this task).
- **Terminology-collision risk flagged:** "verified"/"verification" already has three unrelated meanings in this repository (purchase verification, product branding, identity verification) — disambiguation recommended for any future Founder-/customer-facing `FD-IDENTITY-001`/ITM communication, not a repository defect requiring correction now.
- **Files created:** [`FD-IDENTITY-001-impact-assessment-and-migration-plan-2026-08-01.md`](../00-governance/decisions/evidence/FD-IDENTITY-001-impact-assessment-and-migration-plan-2026-08-01.md) (full analysis); [`FD-IDENTITY-001-founder-decision-package-2026-08-01.md`](../00-governance/decisions/evidence/FD-IDENTITY-001-founder-decision-package-2026-08-01.md) (ready-to-countersign package with drafted amendment text and a proposed `DEC-IDENTITY-001` Decision Register entry); [`IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001-implementation-report-2026-08-01.md`](../05-implementation/reports/IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001-implementation-report-2026-08-01.md) (this task's implementation report). **No other file was created, modified, or deleted** — no application code, PRD, TRD, Decision Register entry, Canonical Reference, Platform Constitution, `CDR-001`, Engineering Implementation Programme, or RTM was edited.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** none to production (no code touched). Documentation risks (terminology collision, gate re-scoping ambiguity, a pre-existing unresolved `DEC-PROV-004`/`DEC-SEC-001` register-mechanical dependency edge, the TRD10 schema gap, governance-artifact-drift precedent) are catalogued in the Impact Assessment §8, not resolved by this task.
- **Rollback:** trivial — this task produced only new, standalone analysis files; deleting them (or `git revert` of this commit) fully reverses it. No governing document was edited, so there is nothing to roll back within them.
- **`EXT-TECH-001` status: unchanged, Still Pending. Capability 2: unchanged, `Blocked`.** `FD-IDENTITY-001`/proposed `DEC-IDENTITY-001`: awaiting Founder countersign.
- **Report links:** [Impact Assessment](../00-governance/decisions/evidence/FD-IDENTITY-001-impact-assessment-and-migration-plan-2026-08-01.md); [Founder Decision Package](../00-governance/decisions/evidence/FD-IDENTITY-001-founder-decision-package-2026-08-01.md); [Implementation Report](../05-implementation/reports/IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001-implementation-report-2026-08-01.md).

## 2026-08-01 — IDENTITY-ALIGN-001: Repository Constitutional Alignment Following FD-IDENTITY-001

- **Context:** the Founder authorized applying the constitutional realignment analyzed under `IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001` (previous entry). Task `IDENTITY-ALIGN-001` is a governance and repository alignment task only — application code was not modified and engineering implementation was not begun.
- **`DEC-IDENTITY-001` recorded** (`CONFIRMED`) in the Decision Register — the Identity, Authentication, Progressive Trust, Standard Participation, Risk-Based Verification, Merchant, and Recovery Principles, per the Founder's task brief. `DEC-PROV-004` and `DEC-SEC-001` amended in place (not superseded) — the exact clauses identified as conflated in the prior entry were replaced with a bracket-marker amendment (`[AMENDED by DEC-IDENTITY-001, 2026-08-01 — see below]`); all other clauses preserved verbatim; pre-amendment text remains intact in each decision's own evidence package and in git history. Decision Register §5 Register Summary: `CONFIRMED` 42 → 43, Total records 103 → 104.
- **Capability architecture restructured, no renumbering:** `CDR-001` §5 Capability 2 now names three architectural concerns — Customer Identity, Authentication, and **Identity Trust Management (ITM)**, an internal-only engineering name (never customer-facing). Capability 2 keeps its number and position; no other capability was renumbered — a new numbered capability was deliberately not introduced, since the split is architectural, not a new customer-observable delivery step, and renumbering would have rippled into the RTM/Programme/Prompt Register/Gate for no sequencing benefit.
- **`EXT-TECH-001` reclassified:** no longer an unconditional Capability Authorisation Gate blocker for `ENG-P2-001`'s baseline Customer Identity work (standard participation no longer requires phone verification); now scoped to the phone-OTP authentication provider's production activation and ITM's phone-verification trust signal. External Dependencies Register, `ENG-P2-RES-000` §7 Gate item 1 (amended in place, same bracket-marker pattern), Engineering Implementation Programme, and Coding-Agent Prompt Register all updated to match. `DEC-PROD-012` is now the sole remaining Capability Authorisation Gate item; `ENG-P2-001` remains `Blocked`.
- **Governing-document wording corrected:** PRD2 §5 Steps 2–4 (previously "Enter mobile number → Verify number → Create account," the passage the prior entry identified as the single most directly contradicted in the repository) and §7 Account Status (`Pending Verification` removed as an identity-gating status, replaced with a transient `Registering` state; trust level reframed as a separate internal ITM signal); TRD12 §12.4.1 (provider hierarchy — "preferred"/"future" — reframed to equal supported providers); Canonical Reference §10 MVP Boundaries (separated the permanent identity triad from authentication-provider choice). RTM swept for the same conflation; no row required correction (`FR-CI-004`/`AIR-003` already correctly state phone changes don't affect identity).
- **Tracker synchronisation:** Engineering Implementation Programme, Master Workflow (using its own established "Updated [date]" + nested `<details>` historical-status pattern), and Coding-Agent Prompt Register all updated to reference `DEC-IDENTITY-001` and the `EXT-TECH-001` reclassification. No work-package status was advanced — `ENG-P2-001`/`ENG-P2-004` remain `Blocked`; no implementation began.
- **`IDENTITY-STRATEGY-001`/PR #53 naming superseded:** this task's authoritative text names the internal capability "Identity Trust Management (ITM)," not "Trust Lifecycle Management (TLM)" (the name used throughout the not-yet-merged PR #53, itself following an earlier Founder recommendation). All 5 PR #53 files renamed TLM→ITM so nothing inconsistent reaches `main`; both evidence documents' status headers updated to record that `DEC-IDENTITY-001` is now recorded and applied.

No application code, no other PRD/TRD, and no historical report under `/reports/`, `/records/`, or a frozen `/evidence/` package (other than the two named above, not yet historical since PR #53 is unmerged) was modified.

## 2026-08-02 — ENG-P2-ARCH-001: Customer Identity Architecture Definition

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `IDENTITY-ALIGN-001` (PR #53, merge commit `226ebbb3650db711483ae1319013be6a66b5f4e8`), the Founder requested the engineering architecture for the newly separated Customer Identity concern. Architecture-definition task only — no production code, database implementation, API, UI, authentication, or trust logic implemented.
- **Identity Aggregate defined:** Internal Customer ID, Loyalty Number, QR Identifier, Profile, Status, and reference-only pointers to Trust (ITM) and Authentication — explicitly excluding authentication credentials/tokens (already excluded by TRD10's own Rules) and verification/trust state (owned by ITM).
- **Identity Lifecycle defined:** Guest → Registered → Active → Dormant → Recovered → Closed (→ Archived), with each transition explained and reconciled against the existing operational states (`Suspended`/`Locked` as orthogonal risk-response states; `Archived` as `Closed`'s terminal state, matching PRD2 §7/TRD10 exactly). `Dormant` and `Recovered` are identified as new states requiring a future TRD10 schema/PRD2 status-model addition — not performed by this task.
- **Loyalty Number and QR lifecycles defined:** generation, uniqueness, permanence, replacement (exceptional-only, not designed), retirement, and restoration rules for both, grounded in `DEC-DATA-007`, `DEC-ID-001`, PRD2 §8–9, and TRD12 `AIR-003`.
- **Identity Recovery defined:** restores history/purchases/rewards/loyalty number/QR/trust state; never creates a replacement identity — directly quoting `DEC-SEC-001`'s 8 Identity Recovery Principles and TRD12 §12.30–31.
- **Authentication and ITM relationship boundaries defined:** "Authentication provides access, does not own identity" (§7, grounded in `DEC-PROV-004`); "Trust strengthens identity, never creates identity" (§8, grounded in `DEC-IDENTITY-001`'s Progressive Trust and Risk-Based Verification Principles).
- **Capability relationship model produced:** Customer Identity → Authentication → ITM → Reward Engine → Recognition → Future capabilities, mapped onto `CDR-001`'s existing, unrenumbered capability sequence.
- **Downstream engineering dependencies identified, not redesigned:** `ENG-P2-001` future decomposition, `ENG-P2-004`, a future Authentication-provider design task, a future ITM design task, a future TRD10 schema-design task (dormant/recovered states, trust/authentication reference fields), `ENG-P4-001`–`ENG-P8-002` (must key against the Loyalty Number, never authentication/trust state), a future Identity Recovery implementation task, a future QR-regeneration implementation task.
- **Files created:** [`ENG-P2-ARCH-001-customer-identity-architecture.md`](../05-implementation/roadmap/ENG-P2-ARCH-001-customer-identity-architecture.md). **Files modified (single-line cross-references only, matching the existing Engineering-Blueprint cross-reference precedent):** `CDR-001` §5 Capability 2 (pointer to the new architecture doc); Engineering Implementation Programme `ENG-P2-001` Current Status (same pointer). No other file touched — no application code, no schema, no PRD/TRD content edit.
- **Dependencies added:** none. **Configuration changes:** none.
- **Risks:** the concrete decomposition of `ENG-P2-001` along the three architectural concerns is still future work; the `Dormant`/`Recovered` lifecycle states have no schema representation yet; the Loyalty Number/QR replacement-rule "exceptional administrative action" case is named but not policy-designed.
- **Rollback:** `git revert` of this task's commit(s), or discard the branch — not yet merged. No code, data, or live configuration affected.
- **Report links:** [`ENG-P2-ARCH-001-customer-identity-architecture.md`](../05-implementation/roadmap/ENG-P2-ARCH-001-customer-identity-architecture.md) (architecture document, doubling as this task's primary deliverable and report per its own §11 Validation Results).

## 2026-08-02 — ENG-P2-001-PLAN-001: Customer Identity Engineering Decomposition

- **Context:** following the Founder-authorized merge of `ENG-P2-ARCH-001` (PR #54, merge commit `0f696af4c5ff693cf0d557b1d58736d641b1157a`), the Founder requested a bounded engineering decomposition of `ENG-P2-001`. Planning task only — no application code, database schema, Firebase Rule, API, UI, authentication-provider, ITM, or reward implementation performed.
- **Decomposition produced:** 10 proposed child work packages under the existing `ENG-P2-001` row — `ENG-P2-001-01` Identity Domain Foundation, `-02` Customer Profile, `-03` Loyalty Number Service, `-04` QR Identity Service, `-05` Identity Persistence, `-06` Identity Lifecycle and Status Management, `-07` Identity Recovery, `-08` Identity Linking and Duplicate Prevention, `-09` Identity Query and Lookup Interfaces, `-10` Identity Audit and Observability — each with explicit scope, exclusions, dependencies, tests required, and exit criteria.
- **Recommended sequence:** `-01` first (root of the dependency graph); `-02`/`-03` in parallel; `-04`; `-05` (integration point); `-06`/`-09` in parallel; `-07`/`-08` in parallel; `-10` last. Three parallelisation opportunities identified.
- **Authentication and ITM interface boundaries defined:** four boundary points each way (authenticated subject reference, method-link event, access context, unlink restrictions, recovery handoff for Authentication; trust reference, verification-state reference, risk-based decision, verification-event reference for ITM) — no provider- or verification-specific logic designed on either side.
- **`DEC-PROD-012` impact assessed, not resolved:** its own Decision Register scope ("profile schema freeze") blocks only the `-02` `gender` field, but `ENG-P2-RES-000` §7 Gate item 6's literal wording is a blanket precondition for `ENG-P2-001` as a whole — flagged as an unresolved tension requiring Founder input, not decided by this task. Capability 2 is **not** marked implementation-ready.
- **Decision and Ambiguity Register (4 items):** (1) `Recovered` lifecycle-state representation — engineering-level, recommended resolution stated, no Founder input required; (2) the `DEC-PROD-012`/Gate-scope tension above — Founder input required; (3) Loyalty Number/QR exceptional-replacement policy — named, not currently blocking; (4) automatic identity-merge authority — explicitly not designed per this task's own constraint, a future decision task required before `-08`'s merge-execution scope can extend beyond detection.
- **Recommended first executable task:** `ENG-P2-001-01` (Identity Domain Foundation) — zero decision dependencies of its own, pure domain code, lowest-risk first slice. Requires a fresh Founder authorization to begin (not granted by this planning task) and, per the Gate ambiguity above, either `DEC-PROD-012`'s resolution or an explicit narrowing of Gate item 6's scope.
- **UX dependencies:** no Stitch concept exists for Registration, Profile, Loyalty Number/QR display, or Recovery screens (a pre-existing, disclosed `CDR-001` gap) — none of this blocks `-01`–`-05`, `-08`, or `-10` (backend-only); customer-facing UI for `-02`/`-06`/`-07`/`-09` needs a future UX pass before implementation.
